fill: rgb(255, 255, 255); font-weight: 300; font-size: 16px; --darkreader-inline-fill: var(--darkreader-text-ffffff, #979694);" data-darkreader-inline-fill=""> </text></svg><div data-v-6f9d6be7="" class="ripple-container mdl-js-ripple-effect mdl-js-button" data-upgraded=",MaterialButton,MaterialRipple"><span class="mdl-button__ripple-container"><span class="mdl-ripple"></span></span></div><div data-v-6f9d6be7="" class="conversation-text conversation-text-small"><div data-v-6f9d6be7="" class="conversation-title-container"><!----><span data-v-6f9d6be7="" class="conversation-title mdl-card__supporting-text conversation-title-small"><!---->Felix Morio</span><!----></div><span data-v-6f9d6be7="" class="conversation-snippet mdl-card__supporting-text conversation-snippet-small">I like the compiler comparison.  That makes sense.  I don't know that I'll ever get over the environment that I loved so much back in the day.  It was so awesome to use rspec (!), watch ci, use pivotal tracker....I just mourn the apparent death of that environment.  For the two years I practiced law in New Zealand, I focused on technology law, so that was actually a good combination for me.  One of my big matters for example was a national bank outsourcing their tech to AWS.  It would be fun to work together again.  Who knows!  Right now I'm just trying to figure out how to not end up on the street.  I was counting on always having tech money as a backup.  Yeah most people don't know much about Mormons.  While there is a religious component, I think a more complete way to look at it is really that it's a culture, and a very specific one.  It's not like being catholic or Protestant and that being sort of separate from your daily life.  It's a constant part of your life every day and also a lifestyle.  Lots of people in dresses and suits here.  No tattoos, piercings, coffee, alcohol, marriage for eternity and not just until death, and no professional clergy.  I live in Provo, which is where one of the church affiliated universities is (BYU).  Family is the most important aspect of the faith and so there's so much about that.  It's very disorienting to see the thousands of married 18 year olds with kids!  No professional clergy means that everything is run by the members.  And there is still a large influence of the persecutions of the 1800s, which caused the trek from NY and IL to Utah in the first place.  But yes--it's isolated here, and some of the social aspects aren't really me.  Very very few professional women, for example.  They're all looking for a worthy priesthood holder (men) to preside over the family they want to have.  It's less extreme outside of Utah, but it's definitely not diverse here!  You might like it here!  There's amazing scenery, it's extremely family friendly, and it's very safe.  Even though that's not the impression outsiders have, people here are extremely tolerant of different views.  They might disagree, but they'll be nice about it, so I think you could pick and choose what you would like!  I'm glad you've prepared for things!  And thanks for the positive wishes.  I'm sure you'll do great!!</span></div></div><div data-v-6f9d6be7="" data-v-0a5ab108="" id="245897950453969" data-timestamp="1772031982000" class="conversation-card mdl-card mdl-js-button Create a new bookmark with this URL:

```javascript
javascript:void(function(){var c=document.querySelector('#toolbar-title');var name=c?c.textContent.trim():'Unknown';var msgs=document.querySelectorAll('.message-wrapper');var lines=['Conversation with: '+name,'','---',''];msgs.forEach(function(w){var m=w.querySelector('.message.sent,.message.received');if(!m)return;var dir=m.classList.contains('sent')?'Me':'Them';var textEl=m.querySelector('div[data-v-3dce3acf]');var text=textEl?textEl.textContent.trim():'';var ts=w.querySelector('.date-sent');var time=ts?ts.textContent.trim():'';if(text)lines.push('['+time+'] '+dir+': '+text);});var blob=new Blob([lines.join('\n')],{type:'text/plain'});var a=document.createElement('a');a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);a.download=name.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g,'_')+'_conversation.txt';a.click()})()
```

**How to use:**
1. Copy the `javascript:void(...)` line above
2. Create a new bookmark in your browser, paste it as the URL
3. Open a conversation in PulseSMS web (app.pulsesms.com)
4. Click the bookmarklet — it downloads a `.txt` file

**Output format:**
```
Conversation with: +1 347-506-4236

---

[Tue 3:10 PM] Them: Hi Brian, ...
[Tue 3:12 PM] Me: Hi Ben, it's Brian Takita...
```

**Notes:**
- Extracts contact name from the toolbar
- Labels messages as "Me" (sent) or "Them" (received)
- Includes timestamps from `.date-sent` elements
- Downloads as `<contact>_conversation.txt`
